A key element of these programmes is the development

of learners' vocabulary knowledge. This involves explicit

- pre-teaching key words before reading a text
- providing glossaries with reading texts
- doing practice activities using the target vocabulary
- teaching techniques that help with memorising new
words.

#### Lesson 1 (Pupil's Book p.70)

Unit 10 At the funfair
Pupil's Book p.70
113
Objectives
Suzy: Look at me, Mum! I'm in the blue helicopter.
Pupils name and talk about means of transport.
Mrs Star: That's nice, Suzy! Where's Stella?
Suzy: She's in the plane.
Target language
Mrs Star: Oh, yes.
Key language: transport: boat, bus, helicopter, lorry.
Simon: Mum, can I ride on the black motorbike?

#### Consolidation activity 1:Colourwords

Materials:Mini whiteboards orA4pieces of paper, one per
team
Pupils work in teams and choose a team name. Give each group a
mini whiteboard or some paper. Say a colour. The teams have 30
seconds to write down things that are that colour.(For example,
grey: elephant, hippo, computer, mouse.) Teams hold up their lists
and saythewords.They get one pointforeach correct answer.
Repeat with another colour.

#### Consolidation activity 2: Wordsnake

Materials:A4 piece of paper for each pair
Pupils work in pairs. They make a list of words for things they can
see in thepicture inActivity 2.They draw the outline of a snake on a
piece of paper and write the words in it as a wordsnake. Pairs swap
papers and circle the words in the snakes.

**Page 100**


#### Consolidation activity:Classroom counting

Write a list of thingsin the classroom on the board:table,chair,
door, bag, boy, girl, animal, computer, book, pencil, teacher. Pupils
work in small teams to copy the list.They thenrace to count how
many of eachobject there areinthe classroom.Feed backwith the
class, asking, e.g. How many tables are there? Teams get a point for
eachcorrectanswer.

#### Extension activity:Talkingpictures

Pupils work in pairs. Each pupil chooses a picture in their Pupil's
Book in which children are doing different activities.Pupil A studies
the picture for 30 seconds, then passes the book to Pupil B. Pupil A
describes the picture to Pupil B from memory. Pairs swap roles.

**Page 101**


#### Consolidation activity 1:Sharkfin

Draw a wiggly line with eight waves, to represent the sea. In the
waveonthefarleft,drawasharkfin.Attheendofthesea,on
the right-hand side, draw a person swimming. Choose a four-letter
word that pupilshave read in the lesson.Write four letter blanks
on the board and ask pupils to guess letters. They say the letter
soundsrather than the names as they don't yetknow the alphabet
